Michael Peña fancy dress face mask

Fancy dress made easy: a Hoshi face mask on thick 350gsm card, Ready Cut or DIY, with elastic or a stick. Great for a karaoke night. Posted flat.

Our Hoshi face mask is a quick way to get everyone laughing at a karaoke night, and it's a fun surprise for K-pop fans. It shows Hoshi from SEVENTEEN printed on sturdy card.

Hoshi fancy dress face mask

Printed in full colour on 350gsm silk card, the mask comes in two styles: Ready Cut (cut to the face shape with the eye holes cut) or DIY (printed only, for you to cut out). Then pick your fitting: elastic and sticky tabs, or a stick and stickers for a hand-held mask, supplied for you to attach. Need a face we don't stock, such as the groom or a friend? Send us a photo and we'll make a custom mask.

Why you'll love it

  • Full A4 size (297 x 210 mm), big enough to cover an adult face
  • An easy, cheap way to theme a karaoke night
  • Brilliant for photo booths and group shots
  • Thick 350gsm silk card holds its shape all night
  • Semi-waterproof finish, so it copes with spilt drinks and outdoor parties

Size & details

  • Size: 297 x 210 mm (A4), a full adult-size face
  • Material: 350gsm silk card, full-colour digital print
  • Finish: semi-waterproof
  • Style: Ready Cut (cut to shape, eye holes cut) or DIY (printed only)
  • Fitting: elastic and sticky tabs, or a stick and stickers, supplied for you to attach
  • Packaging: board-backed envelope

Delivery

Printed in our North Yorkshire workshop and posted to you in a board-backed envelope. Dispatch and postage options are shown at checkout.
